    bool _ne_phasePullback = ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Pre-Convexity" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Induction" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Liquidity"
    bool _ne_phaseDemandReturn = ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return"
    bool _ne_phaseSupplyReturn = ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return"
    float _ne_htfScore = mce_htfAlignmentScore > 0 ? mce_htfAlignmentScore : (htfAlign == displayWaveDir and displayWaveDir != 0 ? 70.0 : 0.0)
    string ne_dominantNarrative =
         displayWaveDir == 0 ? "No Clear Narrative" :
         displayWaveDir == 1  and _ne_phaseExpBull and rot_transferProbability < 40 ? "Bullish Continuation" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 and _ne_phaseExpBear and rot_transferProbability < 40 ? "Bearish Continuation" :
         displayWaveDir == 1  and (_ne_phasePullback or _ne_phaseDemandReturn) ? "Bullish Pullback" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 and (_ne_phasePullback or _ne_phaseSupplyReturn) ? "Bearish Pullback" :
         displayWaveDir == 1  and (rot_transferProbability >= 55 or ie1a_currentPhase == "Absorption") ? "Bullish Reversal Developing" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 and (rot_transferProbability >= 55 or ie1a_currentPhase == "Absorption") ? "Bearish Reversal Developing" :
         displayWaveDir == 1  ? "Bullish Developing" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 ? "Bearish Developing" : "No Clear Narrative"
    float ne_narrativeStrength = math.min(mce_alignmentScore * 0.40 + rot_controlStability * 0.30 + (re_resolutionState == "RESOLVED" ? 30.0 : re_resolutionState == "PARTIALLY RESOLVED" ? 15.0 : 0.0), 100.0)

    // ── V72.5 Invalidation Engine (C9) ───────────────────────────────────────
    float inv_bullOriginPrice = not na(point4OriginLow)  ? point4OriginLow  - atr * invOriginBuffer : na
    float inv_bearOriginPrice = not na(point4OriginHigh) ? point4OriginHigh + atr * invOriginBuffer : na
    float inv_demandFailPrice = direction == 1  and not na(flipBot) ? flipBot - atr * invZoneBuffer : na
    float inv_supplyFailPrice = direction == -1 and not na(flipTop) ? flipTop + atr * invZoneBuffer : na
    float inv_activeStop = direction == 1 ? nz(inv_demandFailPrice, inv_bullOriginPrice) : direction == -1 ? nz(inv_supplyFailPrice, inv_bearOriginPrice) : na
    bool  inv_invalidated = not na(inv_activeStop) and (direction == 1 ? close < inv_activeStop : direction == -1 ? close > inv_activeStop : false)
    float inv_riskInPts = not na(inv_activeStop) ? math.abs(close - inv_activeStop) : na

    // ── V72.6 Target Engine (C2) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    float eae_tertiaryAttractorPrice =
         direction == 1  ? math.max(nz(eae_primaryAttractorPrice, close), nz(eae_secondaryAttractorPrice, close)) + atr * teTertiaryATR :
         direction == -1 ? math.min(nz(eae_primaryAttractorPrice, close), nz(eae_secondaryAttractorPrice, close)) - atr * teTertiaryATR : na
    float eae_tertiaryAttractorScore = re_residualEnergyScore * 0.30 + (mce_htfAlignmentScore >= 75 ? 40.0 : mce_htfAlignmentScore * 0.30) + (re_resolutionState == "RESOLVED" ? 30.0 : 10.0)
    float te_tp1 = na
    int   _te_bestSc = -1
    int   _te_n = array.size(frz_scoreArr)
    if _te_n > 0 and direction != 0
        for _j = 0 to _te_n - 1
            if _j < array.size(frz_statusArr) and _j < array.size(frz_dirArr) and _j < array.size(frz_topArr) and _j < array.size(frz_botArr)
                string _st2 = array.get(frz_statusArr, _j)
                int    _fd3 = array.get(frz_dirArr,    _j)
                if (_st2 == "Open" or _st2 == "Partial") and _fd3 == direction
                    float _mid2 = (array.get(frz_topArr, _j) + array.get(frz_botArr, _j)) / 2.0
                    bool  _beyond = direction == 1 ? _mid2 > close : _mid2 < close
                    int   _sc2 = array.get(frz_scoreArr, _j)
                    if _beyond and _sc2 > _te_bestSc
                        _te_bestSc := _sc2
                        te_tp1 := _mid2
    bool  te_tp2_valid = not na(eae_primaryAttractorPrice) and (direction == 1 ? eae_primaryAttractorPrice > close : direction == -1 ? eae_primaryAttractorPrice < close : false)
    float te_tp2 = te_tp2_valid ? eae_primaryAttractorPrice : na
    bool  te_tp3_valid = teTertiaryOn and not na(eae_tertiaryAttractorPrice) and (direction == 1 ? eae_tertiaryAttractorPrice > close : direction == -1 ? eae_tertiaryAttractorPrice < close : false)
    float te_tp3 = te_tp3_valid ? eae_tertiaryAttractorPrice : na
    float te_tp1_quality = math.min(eae_secondaryAttractorScore * 0.40 + (frz_attractorConvergence ? 40.0 : frz_bestScore * 0.25) + re_recursiveCompletionScore * 0.15 + mce_execAlignmentScore * 0.20, 100.0)
    float te_tp2_quality = math.min(eae_primaryAttractorScore * 0.50 + re_residualEnergyScore * 0.30 + mce_htfAlignmentScore * 0.20, 100.0)
    float te_tp3_quality = math.min((eae_tertiaryAttractorScore > 0 ? eae_tertiaryAttractorScore * 0.60 : 20.0) + (mce_htfAlignmentScore >= 80 ? 40.0 : mce_htfAlignmentScore * 0.30), 100.0)
    float te_rr_tp1 = (not na(te_tp1) and not na(inv_riskInPts) and inv_riskInPts > 0) ? math.abs(te_tp1 - close) / inv_riskInPts : na
    bool  te_rrGate = not na(te_rr_tp1) and te_rr_tp1 >= rrMinimum
    string te_expectedPath =
         re_resolutionState == "RESOLVED" and mce_execAlignmentScore >= 70 ? "Direct" :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return") ? "Direct" :
         rot_transferProbability > 50 ? "Retracement First" :
         re_resolutionState == "UNRESOLVED" ? "Range Then Breakout" : "Direct"

    // ── V72.7 Trade Qualification Engine (C4, C6) ────────────────────────────
    float erf_pureConfidence = math.min(20.0 + (re_resolutionState == "RESOLVED" ? 30.0 : re_resolutionState == "PARTIALLY RESOLVED" ? 20.0 : 10.0) + eae_primaryAttractorScore * 0.30, 100.0)
    float frz_entryBonus = (frz_inProximity and frz_bestZoneDir == direction) ? 15.0 : 0.0
    float tqe_frzQuality = frz_activeCount > 0 ? math.min(frz_bestScore * 0.50 + (frz_bestZoneTier == "T1" ? 40.0 : frz_bestZoneTier == "T2" ? 25.0 : frz_bestZoneTier == "T3" ? 10.0 : 0.0) + (frz_bestStatus == "Open" ? 10.0 : frz_bestStatus == "Partial" ? 5.0 : 0.0) + frz_entryBonus, 100.0) : 0.0
    float tqe_liqQuality = math.min(obs_LiquidityScore * 0.50 + ((liqSweepBull or liqSweepBear) ? 30.0 : 0.0) + (liqVacuum ? 20.0 : 0.0), 100.0)
    float tqe_rawScore = ie1a_phaseConfidence * tqeW_ie1a + erf_pureConfidence * tqeW_erf + tqe_frzQuality * tqeW_frz + mce_htfAlignmentScore * tqeW_mce + re_recursiveCompletionScore * tqeW_re + tqe_liqQuality * tqeW_liq
    string tqe_grade = tqe_rawScore >= tqeGradeA1 ? "A+" : tqe_rawScore >= tqeGradeA ? "A" : tqe_rawScore >= tqeGradeB ? "B" : tqe_rawScore >= tqeGradeC ? "C" : "D"
    string tqe_riskLevel = (rot_transferProbability > 65 or re_resolutionState == "UNRESOLVED") ? "HIGH" : (rot_transferProbability > 35 or tqe_rawScore < 60) ? "MEDIUM" : "LOW"

    // ── V72.8 Decision Output Engine (C1, C6) ────────────────────────────────
    string doe_bias =
         displayWaveDir == 1  and mce_htfAlignmentScore >= 75 and rot_controlStability >= 65 ? "Strong Bullish" :
         displayWaveDir == 1  ? "Bullish" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 and mce_htfAlignmentScore >= 75 and rot_controlStability >= 65 ? "Strong Bearish" :
         displayWaveDir == -1 ? "Bearish" : "Neutral"
    string doe_action =
         inv_invalidated ? "No Trade" :
         not erf_entryGate ? "Wait" :
         not te_rrGate ? "Wait" :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" and direction == 1)  ? "Long"  :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return" and direction == -1) ? "Short" : "Wait"
    float doe_confidence = math.min(modelConfidence * 0.50 + mce_alignmentScore * 0.25 + ie1a_phaseConfidence * 0.25, frz_attractorConvergence ? doeCapConv : doeCapBase)
    string doe_tradeType =
         (ne_dominantNarrative == "Bullish Continuation" or ne_dominantNarrative == "Bearish Continuation") ? "Continuation" :
         (ne_dominantNarrative == "Bullish Pullback"     or ne_dominantNarrative == "Bearish Pullback")     ? "Pullback" :
         (ne_dominantNarrative == "Bullish Rotation"     or ne_dominantNarrative == "Bearish Rotation")     ? "Rotation" :
         ne_dominantNarrative == "Range Development" ? "Range" :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "New High" or ie1a_currentPhase == "New Low") ? "Breakout" : "Wait"
    float doe_entryMid =
         (frz_inProximity and frz_bestZoneDir == direction and not na(frz_bestZoneMid)) ? frz_bestZoneMid :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" and not na(point4OriginHigh) and not na(point4OriginLow)) ? (point4OriginHigh + point4OriginLow) / 2.0 :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return" and not na(point4OriginHigh) and not na(point4OriginLow)) ? (point4OriginHigh + point4OriginLow) / 2.0 : close
    float doe_entryHigh = not na(doe_entryMid) ? doe_entryMid + atr * 0.30 : na
    float doe_entryLow  = not na(doe_entryMid) ? doe_entryMid - atr * 0.30 : na
    string doe_entryTrigger = frz_inProximity ? "Limit" : (ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return") ? "LimitOnRetest" : "Market"

    // ── V72.8b OPPORTUNITY PROGRESS ENGINE (Phase 1) ─────────────────────────
    // Direction = displayWaveDir (single source). Progress SYNTHESIZES engine
    // states; it never decides direction. Hard vetoes (no direction / invalidated)
    // force Stand Down; ERF/HTF/phase/MCE are graded contributors, not vetoes.
    float _opStruct    = displayWaveDir == 0 ? 0.0 : (direction == displayWaveDir ? 100.0 : 70.0)
    float _opLifecycle =
         ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return" ? 100.0 :
         ie1a_currentPhase == "Absorption" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Pre-Convexity" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Induction" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement Liquidity" ? 70.0 :
         ie1a_currentPhase == "New High" or ie1a_currentPhase == "New Low" ? 55.0 :
         ie1a_currentPhase == "Expansion" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Expansion Pre-Convexity" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Expansion Induction" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Expansion Liquidity" ? 45.0 :
         ie1a_currentPhase == "Point 4 Origin" ? 25.0 : 0.0
    float _opRotation   = rot_transferProbability
    float _opReturn     = na(frz_distanceToZone) ? (frz_inProximity ? 100.0 : 0.0) : math.max(0.0, 100.0 - frz_distanceToZone * 40.0)
    float _opHtf        = mce_htfAlignmentScore
    float _opDisplace   = math.max(re_recursiveCompletionScore, ede_dissipationProgress)
    float _opConfluence = (frz_attractorConvergence ? 50.0 : 0.0) + ((frz_inProximity and frz_bestZoneDir == displayWaveDir) ? 50.0 : 0.0)
    float oppProgressRaw = _opStruct * 0.25 + _opLifecycle * 0.15 + _opRotation * 0.15 + _opReturn * 0.15 + _opHtf * 0.10 + _opDisplace * 0.10 + _opConfluence * 0.10
    bool  oppVeto     = displayWaveDir == 0 or inv_invalidated
    float oppProgress = oppVeto ? 0.0 : oppProgressRaw
    bool  _opInZone   = frz_inProximity and frz_bestZoneDir == displayWaveDir
    string oppState =
         oppVeto                                ? "STAND DOWN" :
         oppProgress >= 95 and _opInZone        ? "TRADE ACTIVE" :
         oppProgress >= 85                      ? "PREPARE TO EXECUTE" :
         oppProgress >= 60                      ? "OPPORTUNITY CONFIRMING" :
         oppProgress >= 20                      ? "OPPORTUNITY FORMING" : "STAND DOWN"

    // Synthesized intelligence narrative (consumes displayWaveDir + lifecycle + rotation)
    string _cmdDirWord = displayWaveDir == 1 ? "Bullish" : displayWaveDir == -1 ? "Bearish" : "Neutral"
    string cmd_narrative =
         displayWaveDir == 0 ? "No directional thesis" :
         inv_invalidated ? _cmdDirWord + " thesis invalidated" :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Demand Return" or ie1a_currentPhase == "Supply Return") ? _cmdDirWord + " return-to-zone — execution window" :
         rot_transferProbability >= 55 or ie1a_currentPhase == "Absorption" ? _cmdDirWord + " reversal developing" :
         (_ne_phasePullback or ie1a_currentPhase == "Retracement") ? _cmdDirWord + " pullback in progress" :
         (ie1a_currentPhase == "Expansion" or ie1a_currentPhase == "New High" or ie1a_currentPhase == "New Low") ? _cmdDirWord + " continuation" :
         _cmdDirWord + " developing"

    // ── V72.9 Wave Registry (C5, C12) — var locals persist (single call site) ─
    var array<int>    wr_waveId          = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    wr_parentWaveId    = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    wr_rootWaveId      = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    wr_birthBar        = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    wr_deathBar        = array.new_int()
    var array<string> wr_spawnPhase      = array.new_string()
    var array<string> wr_deathPhase      = array.new_string()
    var array<float>  wr_peakEnergy      = array.new_float()
    var array<float>  wr_resolutionScore = array.new_float()
    var array<int>    wr_waveDepth       = array.new_int()
    var int wr_nextId         = 1
    var int wr_activeWaveId   = 0
    var int wr_activeParentId = 0
    var int wr_activeRootId   = 0
    int     wr_maxEntries     = 50
    int  _wr_prevDir   = nz(direction[1])
    int  _wr_prevCycle = nz(entryCycle[1])
    bool _wr_died     = direction == 0 and _wr_prevDir != 0
    bool _wr_newRoot  = (direction != 0 and _wr_prevDir == 0) or (direction != 0 and _wr_prevDir != 0 and direction != _wr_prevDir)
    bool _wr_newChild = direction != 0 and entryCycle > _wr_prevCycle and not _wr_newRoot
    if _wr_died and wr_activeWaveId > 0
        int _di = array.indexof(wr_waveId, wr_activeWaveId)
        if _di >= 0
            array.set(wr_deathBar,        _di, bar_index)
            array.set(wr_deathPhase,      _di, ie1a_currentPhase)
            array.set(wr_resolutionScore, _di, re_recursiveCompletionScore)
        wr_activeWaveId   := 0
        wr_activeParentId := 0
        wr_activeRootId   := 0
    if _wr_newRoot or _wr_newChild
        int _newId  = wr_nextId
        wr_nextId  := wr_nextId + 1
        int _parent = _wr_newChild ? wr_activeWaveId : 0
        int _root   = _wr_newChild and wr_activeRootId > 0 ? wr_activeRootId : _newId
        int _depth  = _wr_newChild ? math.max(0, entryCycle) : 0
        array.push(wr_waveId,          _newId)
        array.push(wr_parentWaveId,    _parent)
        array.push(wr_rootWaveId,      _root)
        array.push(wr_birthBar,        bar_index)
        array.push(wr_deathBar,        -1)
        array.push(wr_spawnPhase,      ie1a_currentPhase)
        array.push(wr_deathPhase,      "")
        array.push(wr_peakEnergy,      ede_expansionEnergy)
        array.push(wr_resolutionScore, 0.0)
        array.push(wr_waveDepth,       _depth)
        wr_activeWaveId   := _newId
        wr_activeParentId := _parent
        wr_activeRootId   := _root
        if array.size(wr_waveId) > wr_maxEntries
            array.shift(wr_waveId)
            array.shift(wr_parentWaveId)
            array.shift(wr_rootWaveId)
            array.shift(wr_birthBar)
            array.shift(wr_deathBar)
            array.shift(wr_spawnPhase)
            array.shift(wr_deathPhase)
            array.shift(wr_peakEnergy)
            array.shift(wr_resolutionScore)
            array.shift(wr_waveDepth)
    if wr_activeWaveId > 0
        int _ai = array.indexof(wr_waveId, wr_activeWaveId)
        if _ai >= 0 and ede_expansionEnergy > array.get(wr_peakEnergy, _ai)
            array.set(wr_peakEnergy, _ai, ede_expansionEnergy)
    int _wr_depthDisp = 0
    if wr_activeWaveId > 0
        int _aidx = array.indexof(wr_waveId, wr_activeWaveId)
        if _aidx >= 0
            _wr_depthDisp := array.get(wr_waveDepth, _aidx)

    // ── V72.10 Delivery Wave Registry ────────────────────────────────────────
    var array<int>    dwr_waveId          = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    dwr_rootWaveId      = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    dwr_startBar        = array.new_int()
    var array<int>    dwr_endBar          = array.new_int()
    var array<float>  dwr_energy          = array.new_float()
    var array<string> dwr_resolutionState = array.new_string()
    var array<int>    dwr_entryCycle      = array.new_int()
    var int dwr_nextId     = 1
    var int dwr_activeId   = 0
    int     dwr_maxEntries = 50
    var bool _dwr_prevLBD = false
    bool dwr_shouldSpawn = (ede_state == 4 and nz(ede_state[1]) != 4) or (ede_liquidationBecomingDirectional and not _dwr_prevLBD)
    _dwr_prevLBD := ede_liquidationBecomingDirectional
    if dwr_shouldSpawn
        int _did   = dwr_nextId
        dwr_nextId := dwr_nextId + 1
        array.push(dwr_waveId,          _did)
        array.push(dwr_rootWaveId,      wr_activeRootId)
        array.push(dwr_startBar,        bar_index)
        array.push(dwr_endBar,          -1)
        array.push(dwr_energy,          ede_expansionEnergy)
        array.push(dwr_resolutionState, re_resolutionState)
        array.push(dwr_entryCycle,      entryCycle)
        dwr_activeId := _did
        if array.size(dwr_waveId) > dwr_maxEntries
            array.shift(dwr_waveId)
            array.shift(dwr_rootWaveId)
            array.shift(dwr_startBar)
            array.shift(dwr_endBar)
            array.shift(dwr_energy)
            array.shift(dwr_resolutionState)
            array.shift(dwr_entryCycle)
    if dwr_activeId > 0
        int _dwi = array.indexof(dwr_waveId, dwr_activeId)
        if _dwi >= 0
            array.set(dwr_resolutionState, _dwi, re_resolutionState)
            if re_resolutionState == "RESOLVED" and array.get(dwr_endBar, _dwi) == -1
                array.set(dwr_endBar, _dwi, bar_index)

    // ── V72.11 Traceability strings ──────────────────────────────────────────
    string trc_decisionSource = "IE1A:" + ie1a_currentPhase + " | TQE:" + tqe_grade + " | NE:" + ne_dominantNarrative
    string trc_confidenceBreakdown = "IE1A:" + str.tostring(math.round(ie1a_phaseConfidence)) + " ERF:" + str.tostring(math.round(erf_pureConfidence)) + " FRZ:" + str.tostring(math.round(tqe_frzQuality)) + " MCE:" + str.tostring(math.round(mce_htfAlignmentScore)) + " RE:" + str.tostring(math.round(re_recursiveCompletionScore)) + " LIQ:" + str.tostring(math.round(tqe_liqQuality))
    string trc_waveChain = "Wave#" + str.tostring(wr_activeWaveId) + " (parent #" + str.tostring(wr_activeParentId) + ", root #" + str.tostring(wr_activeRootId) + ")"
    string trc_deliveryChain = "Delivery#" + str.tostring(dwr_activeId)

    // ── V72.11.5 TARGET PROJECTION LAYER (TPL) — single live destination ──────
    // Answers "where is price most likely trying to go right now?" by ranking
    // FRZ / EAE attractor / FU imbalance candidates. Direction is the fixed M5
    // structure (displayWaveDir), so the destination is timeframe-independent.
    int   _tpl_bias = displayWaveDir != 0 ? displayWaveDir : fractalStackDir
    // Candidates
    float tpl_frzTarget       = _tpl_bias == 1 ? frz_bestZoneTop : _tpl_bias == -1 ? frz_bestZoneBot : na
    float tpl_attractorTarget = eae_primaryAttractorPrice
    float _tpl_fuTop = array.size(fu_top) > 0 ? array.get(fu_top, array.size(fu_top) - 1) : na
    float _tpl_fuBot = array.size(fu_bot) > 0 ? array.get(fu_bot, array.size(fu_bot) - 1) : na
    float tpl_fuTarget        = _tpl_bias == 1 ? _tpl_fuTop : _tpl_bias == -1 ? _tpl_fuBot : na
    // Scores (0 when a candidate is invalid, so it cannot win)
    // Direction-validate: a LONG destination must be ABOVE price, a SHORT below.
    // Wrong-side levels (e.g. a downside attractor while bullish) are excluded.
    bool _tpl_frzOK = not na(tpl_frzTarget)       and _tpl_bias != 0 and (_tpl_bias == 1 ? tpl_frzTarget       > close : tpl_frzTarget       < close)
    bool _tpl_attOK = not na(tpl_attractorTarget) and _tpl_bias != 0 and (_tpl_bias == 1 ? tpl_attractorTarget > close : tpl_attractorTarget < close)
    bool _tpl_fuOK  = not na(tpl_fuTarget)        and _tpl_bias != 0 and (_tpl_bias == 1 ? tpl_fuTarget        > close : tpl_fuTarget        < close)
    float tpl_frzScore       = (frz_activeCount > 0 and _tpl_frzOK) ? math.min(frz_bestScore * 0.45 + mce_alignmentScore * 0.25 + erf_tradeReadiness * 0.30, 100.0) : 0.0
    float tpl_attractorScore = _tpl_attOK ? math.min(80.0 + erf_tradeReadiness * 0.20, 100.0) : 0.0
    float tpl_fuScore        = _tpl_fuOK  ? math.min(75.0 + tqe_rawScore * 0.25, 100.0) : 0.0
    // Winner
    float  tpl_mainTarget = na
    string tpl_source     = "–"
    float  tpl_confidence = 0.0
    // Winner — PRIORITY order (FRZ is the curated, scored return-zone engine and is
    // the preferred destination): FRZ > Attractor > FU imbalance > projection.
    if _tpl_frzOK and frz_activeCount > 0
        tpl_mainTarget := tpl_frzTarget
        tpl_source     := "FRZ"
        tpl_confidence := tpl_frzScore
    else if _tpl_attOK
        tpl_mainTarget := tpl_attractorTarget
        tpl_source     := "ATTRACTOR"
        tpl_confidence := tpl_attractorScore
    else if _tpl_fuOK
        tpl_mainTarget := tpl_fuTarget
        tpl_source     := "FU IMBALANCE"
        tpl_confidence := tpl_fuScore
    // Fallback: if no structural candidate is available, project from the fixed
    // stack bias so a live destination is ALWAYS shown.
    if na(tpl_mainTarget)
        tpl_mainTarget := _tpl_bias == -1 ? close - atr * 5.0 : close + atr * 5.0
        tpl_source     := "PROJECTION"
        tpl_confidence := 40.0
    // ── Opposite-side destination — where price likely heads if the trade TURNS ──
    int    _tpl_rev      = -_tpl_bias
    float  tpl_revFrz    = _tpl_rev == 1 ? frz_bestZoneTop : _tpl_rev == -1 ? frz_bestZoneBot : na
    float  tpl_revFu     = _tpl_rev == 1 ? _tpl_fuTop : _tpl_rev == -1 ? _tpl_fuBot : na
    bool   _rFrzOK = not na(tpl_revFrz)          and _tpl_rev != 0 and (_tpl_rev == 1 ? tpl_revFrz          > close : tpl_revFrz          < close)
    bool   _rAttOK = not na(tpl_attractorTarget) and _tpl_rev != 0 and (_tpl_rev == 1 ? tpl_attractorTarget > close : tpl_attractorTarget < close)
    bool   _rFuOK  = not na(tpl_revFu)           and _tpl_rev != 0 and (_tpl_rev == 1 ? tpl_revFu           > close : tpl_revFu           < close)
    float  tpl_revTarget = na
    string tpl_revSource = "–"
    if _rFrzOK and frz_activeCount > 0
        tpl_revTarget := tpl_revFrz
        tpl_revSource := "FRZ"
    else if _rAttOK
        tpl_revTarget := tpl_attractorTarget
        tpl_revSource := "ATTRACTOR"
    else if _rFuOK
        tpl_revTarget := tpl_revFu
        tpl_revSource := "FU"
    if na(tpl_revTarget) and _tpl_rev != 0
        tpl_revTarget := _tpl_rev == -1 ? close - atr * 5.0 : close + atr * 5.0
        tpl_revSource := "PROJECTION"
    // Alternative = highest-scoring candidate excluding the winner
    float _tpl_aFrz = tpl_source == "FRZ"          ? -1.0 : tpl_frzScore
    float _tpl_aAtt = tpl_source == "ATTRACTOR"    ? -1.0 : tpl_attractorScore
    float _tpl_aFu  = tpl_source == "FU IMBALANCE" ? -1.0 : tpl_fuScore
    float  tpl_altScore  = math.max(_tpl_aFrz, math.max(_tpl_aAtt, _tpl_aFu))
    float  tpl_altTarget = tpl_altScore <= 0 ? na : tpl_altScore == _tpl_aFrz ? tpl_frzTarget : tpl_altScore == _tpl_aAtt ? tpl_attractorTarget : tpl_fuTarget
    string tpl_altSource = tpl_altScore <= 0 ? "–" : tpl_altScore == _tpl_aFrz ? "FRZ" : tpl_altScore == _tpl_aAtt ? "ATTRACTOR" : "FU IMBALANCE"
    float  tpl_distanceATR = not na(tpl_mainTarget) ? math.abs(close - tpl_mainTarget) / math.max(atr, 1e-10) : na
    string tpl_status = na(tpl_mainTarget) ? "NO TARGET" : tpl_confidence >= 80 ? "MAGNET ACTIVE" : tpl_confidence >= 60 ? "FORMING" : "WEAK"

    // Live destination line + label (single object, refreshed on the last bar)
    var line  tpl_line  = na
    var label tpl_label = na
    if barstate.islast and showV72Destination
        if not na(tpl_line)
            line.delete(tpl_line)
        if not na(tpl_label)
            label.delete(tpl_label)
        if not na(tpl_mainTarget)
            tpl_line := line.new(bar_index, tpl_mainTarget, bar_index + 50, tpl_mainTarget, width=3, color=color.new(color.yellow, 0))
            tpl_label := label.new(bar_index + 50, tpl_mainTarget,
                 "DESTINATION\n" + str.tostring(math.round_to_mintick(tpl_mainTarget)) + "\n" + tpl_source + "  " + str.tostring(math.round(tpl_confidence)) + "%",
                 style=label.style_label_left, color=color.new(#0a0a30, 0), textcolor=color.yellow, size=size.small)

    // Opposite-side destination line (dotted, dim) — shown only if toggled
    var line  tpl_revLine  = na
    var label tpl_revLabel = na
    if barstate.islast and showV72Destination and showV72RevDest
        if not na(tpl_revLine)
            line.delete(tpl_revLine)
        if not na(tpl_revLabel)
            label.delete(tpl_revLabel)
        if not na(tpl_revTarget)
            tpl_revLine := line.new(bar_index, tpl_revTarget, bar_index + 50, tpl_revTarget, width=1, color=color.new(color.orange, 20), style=line.style_dotted)
            tpl_revLabel := label.new(bar_index + 50, tpl_revTarget,
                 "REV DEST\n" + str.tostring(math.round_to_mintick(tpl_revTarget)) + "\n" + tpl_revSource,
                 style=label.style_label_left, color=color.new(#1a0a00, 0), textcolor=color.orange, size=size.small)

    // ── V72 DESTINATION is rendered inside the V72 COMMAND panel below (the
    //    standalone top_left panel was hidden behind the chart legend). ────────

    // ── V72.12 Dashboards ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if barstate.islast and showTable and showV72Command
        var table cmd = table.new(position.middle_right, 2, 28, bgcolor=color.new(#05050f, 0), border_color=color.new(#00ffcc, 40), border_width=1, frame_color=color.new(#00ffcc, 10), frame_width=1)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 0, "V72 COMMAND", text_color=color.white, bgcolor=color.new(#0a0a30, 0), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 0, oppState, text_color=color.black, bgcolor=(oppState == "TRADE ACTIVE" ? #00ff88 : oppState == "PREPARE TO EXECUTE" ? color.lime : oppState == "OPPORTUNITY CONFIRMING" ? color.yellow : oppState == "OPPORTUNITY FORMING" ? color.orange : color.gray), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 1, "Bias", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 1, doe_bias, text_color=(displayWaveDir == 1 ? color.lime : displayWaveDir == -1 ? color.red : color.gray), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 2, "Narrative", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 2, cmd_narrative, text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 3, "Opportunity", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 3, f_v72_pct(oppProgress) + "  " + oppState, text_color=(oppProgress >= 85 ? #00ff88 : oppProgress >= 60 ? color.yellow : oppProgress >= 20 ? color.orange : color.gray), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 4, "Confidence", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 4, f_v72_pct(doe_confidence), text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 5, "Trade Type", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 5, doe_tradeType, text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 6, "Entry Zone", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 6, (oppState == "PREPARE TO EXECUTE" or oppState == "TRADE ACTIVE") ? (f_v72_px(doe_entryLow) + " – " + f_v72_px(doe_entryHigh)) : "–", text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 7, "Entry Trigger", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 7, (oppState == "PREPARE TO EXECUTE" or oppState == "TRADE ACTIVE") ? doe_entryTrigger : "–", text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 8, "Trade Quality", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 8, tqe_grade + " (prov)", text_color=color.black, bgcolor=f_v72_gradeCol(tqe_grade), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 9, "Risk Level", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 9, tqe_riskLevel, text_color=f_v72_riskCol(tqe_riskLevel), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 10, "TP1", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 10, f_v72_px(te_tp1) + "  (" + f_v72_pct(te_tp1_quality) + ")", text_color=color.lime, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 11, "TP2", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 11, f_v72_px(te_tp2) + "  (" + f_v72_pct(te_tp2_quality) + ")", text_color=color.lime, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 12, "TP3", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 12, te_tp3_quality >= 40 ? f_v72_px(te_tp3) + "  (" + f_v72_pct(te_tp3_quality) + ")" : "–", text_color=color.lime, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 13, "Stop", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 13, f_v72_px(inv_activeStop) + (inv_invalidated ? " ✗" : ""), text_color=color.red, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 14, "R:R TP1", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 14, f_v72_num(te_rr_tp1, 2) + (te_rrGate ? " ✓" : " ✗"), text_color=te_rrGate ? color.lime : color.orange, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 15, "Wave Phase", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 15, ie1a_currentPhase + " (" + f_v72_pct(ie1a_phaseConfidence) + ")", text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 16, "ERF Readiness", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 16, f_v72_pct(erf_tradeReadiness) + (erf_entryGate ? " ✓" : " ✗"), text_color=erf_entryGate ? color.lime : color.orange, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 17, "FRZ Distance", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 17, na(frz_distanceToZone) ? "–" : f_v72_num(frz_distanceToZone, 2) + " ATR", text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 18, "Primary Attractor", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 18, na(eae_primaryAttractorPrice) ? "–" : (f_v72_px(eae_primaryAttractorPrice) + "  " + eae_primaryAttractorLabel), text_color=color.aqua, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 19, "Convergence", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 19, frz_attractorConvergence ? "● ACTIVE" : "○ none", text_color=frz_attractorConvergence ? #00ffcc : color.gray, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 20, "HTF Read", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 20, mce_htfAlignmentSummary, text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 21, "Exec Read", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 21, mce_execAlignmentSummary, text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 22, "Stack Align", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 22,
             (fractalStackDir == 1 ? "▲ " : fractalStackDir == -1 ? "▼ " : "– ") +
             str.tostring(math.round(fractalStackScore)) + "%  (" +
             str.tostring(_stackBull) + "B/" + str.tostring(_stackBear) + "S)",
             text_color=fractalStackScore >= 80 ? #00ff88 : fractalStackScore >= 60 ? color.yellow : color.silver,
             text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 23, "DESTINATION", text_color=color.black, bgcolor=color.new(color.yellow, 0), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 23, na(tpl_mainTarget) ? "–" : (f_v72_px(tpl_mainTarget) + "  " + str.tostring(math.round(tpl_confidence)) + "%"), text_color=color.black, bgcolor=(tpl_status == "MAGNET ACTIVE" ? #00ff88 : tpl_status == "FORMING" ? color.yellow : color.gray), text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 24, "Dest Source", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 24, tpl_source, text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 25, "Dest Dist", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 25, na(tpl_distanceATR) ? "–" : (str.tostring(tpl_distanceATR, "#.0") + " ATR  " + tpl_status), text_color=color.white, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 26, "Dest Alt", text_color=#00ffcc, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 26, na(tpl_altTarget) ? "–" : (f_v72_px(tpl_altTarget) + " (" + tpl_altSource + ")"), text_color=color.silver, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 0, 27, "Rev Dest", text_color=color.orange, text_size=_szCmd)
        table.cell(cmd, 1, 27, not showV72RevDest ? "(off)" : na(tpl_revTarget) ? "–" : (f_v72_px(tpl_revTarget) + " (" + tpl_revSource + ")"), text_color=color.orange, text_size=_szCmd)
    0

// ── Run the V72 engine once per bar ──────────────────────────────────────────
f_v72_run(nz(dir_tf3_raw), nz(dir_tf4_raw), v72F, v72I, v72B, v72Sz)



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CONTEXTUAL WAVE ARCS (v90) — restored: L4 (H4) + L2 (H1) big-degree arcs only,
// drawn as curved dashed polylines through each layer's confirmed swing pivots.
// (Finer L1/L0 arcs were dropped to respect Pine's token limit.)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
showCtxArcs = input.bool(true, "Show Contextual Wave Arcs",   group=GRP_VIS)
ctxArcL4    = input.bool(true, "  Arc: L4 (H4)",              group=GRP_VIS)
ctxArcL2    = input.bool(true, "  Arc: L2 (H1)",              group=GRP_VIS)
ctxArcMax   = input.int (14,   "  Arc Pivot Memory (points)", group=GRP_VIS, minval=3, maxval=40)

f_arcPush(array<chart.point> _pts, float _price, int _maxPts) =>
    array.push(_pts, chart.point.from_time(time, _price))
    if array.size(_pts) > _maxPts
        array.shift(_pts)
    array.size(_pts)

var array<chart.point> arcL4pts = array.new<chart.point>()
var polyline arcL4 = na
var float arcL4_shp = na
var float arcL4_slp = na
if showCtxArcs and ctxArcL4
    bool _nH = not na(se240_sh) and se240_sh != arcL4_shp
    bool _nL = not na(se240_sl) and se240_sl != arcL4_slp
    if _nH
        int _i7 = f_arcPush(arcL4pts, se240_sh, ctxArcMax)
    if _nL
        int _i8 = f_arcPush(arcL4pts, se240_sl, ctxArcMax)
    arcL4_shp := se240_sh
    arcL4_slp := se240_sl
    if (_nH or _nL) and array.size(arcL4pts) >= 2
        if not na(arcL4)
            polyline.delete(arcL4)
        arcL4 := polyline.new(arcL4pts, curved=true, xloc=xloc.bar_time, line_color=color.new(color.orange, 15), line_style=line.style_dashed, line_width=3)

var array<chart.point> arcL2pts = array.new<chart.point>()
var polyline arcL2 = na
var float arcL2_shp = na
var float arcL2_slp = na
if showCtxArcs and ctxArcL2
    bool _nH = not na(se60_sh) and se60_sh != arcL2_shp
    bool _nL = not na(se60_sl) and se60_sl != arcL2_slp
    if _nH
        int _i1 = f_arcPush(arcL2pts, se60_sh, ctxArcMax)
    if _nL
        int _i2 = f_arcPush(arcL2pts, se60_sl, ctxArcMax)
    arcL2_shp := se60_sh
    arcL2_slp := se60_sl
    if (_nH or _nL) and array.size(arcL2pts) >= 2
        if not na(arcL2)
            polyline.delete(arcL2)
        arcL2 := polyline.new(arcL2pts, curved=true, xloc=xloc.bar_time, line_color=color.new(color.yellow, 25), line_style=line.style_dashed, line_width=2)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PING PONG MODE (v90)
// Always-in, alternating system: BUY the up-legs, SELL the down-legs, flipping on
// every structure reversal of the selected source. Captures BOTH sides of the
// oscillation instead of waiting for one directional setup. Self-contained and
// off by default — does NOT alter the V72 execution engine; it runs in parallel
// with its own signals, alerts and panel so you can act on every move.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var string GRP_PP = "Ping Pong Mode"
ppMode        = input.bool(false, "Enable Ping Pong Mode",            group=GRP_PP)
ppSource      = input.string("L0 (M5)", "Flip Trigger Source",
     options=["Chart CHoCH", "M1", "L3 (M3)", "L0 (M5)", "L1 (M15)", "L2 (H1)"], group=GRP_PP)
ppShowSignals = input.bool(true,  "Show Ping Pong Signals",          group=GRP_PP)
ppShowPanel   = input.bool(true,  "Show Ping Pong Panel",            group=GRP_PP)
ppAlertOn     = input.bool(true,  "Alert on Ping Pong Flip",         group=GRP_PP)
ppFilter      = input.bool(false, "Filter: only flip WITH fractal-stack direction", group=GRP_PP)
ppTpR         = input.float(2.0,  "Take Profit (R multiple of stop)",group=GRP_PP, minval=0.5, maxval=10.0, step=0.5)

// Chart-CHoCH direction (used only when the source is "Chart CHoCH")
var int pp_chartDir = 0
if bullCHoCH
    pp_chartDir := 1
if bearCHoCH
    pp_chartDir := -1

// Selected structure direction that drives the flips
int pp_srcDir =
     ppSource == "Chart CHoCH" ? pp_chartDir :
     ppSource == "M1"          ? m1_dir      :
     ppSource == "L3 (M3)"     ? l3_dir      :
     ppSource == "L0 (M5)"     ? l0_dir      :
     ppSource == "L1 (M15)"    ? l1_dir      : l2_dir

var int   pp_pos      = 0     // current side: 1 long, -1 short, 0 flat
var float pp_entry    = na    // entry price of the current leg
var int   pp_entryBar = na
var int   pp_flips    = 0
var float pp_cumPts   = 0.0   // realised points captured across all legs
var float pp_lastLeg  = 0.0   // points captured on the last closed leg
var float pp_sl       = na    // current trade stop loss
var float pp_tp       = na    // current trade take profit
var int   pp_wins     = 0
var int   pp_losses   = 0
// Source structural swings (for the stop) — match the flip-trigger source
float pp_swH = ppSource == "M1" ? m1_swH : ppSource == "L3 (M3)" ? l3_swH : ppSource == "L1 (M15)" ? l1_swH : ppSource == "L2 (H1)" ? l2_swH : l0_swH
float pp_swL = ppSource == "M1" ? m1_swL : ppSource == "L3 (M3)" ? l3_swL : ppSource == "L1 (M15)" ? l1_swL : ppSource == "L2 (H1)" ? l2_swL : l0_swL

// Flip when the source direction opposes (or initialises) the current side.
// Optional filter: when on, only flip in the fractal-stack direction (reduces chop).
bool pp_rawFlip = ppMode and pp_srcDir != 0 and pp_srcDir != pp_pos
bool pp_flip    = pp_rawFlip and (pp_pos == 0 or not ppFilter or pp_srcDir == fractalStackDir)
if pp_flip
    if pp_pos != 0 and not na(pp_entry)
        pp_lastLeg := (close - pp_entry) * pp_pos
        pp_cumPts  := pp_cumPts + pp_lastLeg
        if pp_lastLeg > 0
            pp_wins := pp_wins + 1
        else if pp_lastLeg < 0
            pp_losses := pp_losses + 1
    pp_pos      := pp_srcDir
    pp_entry    := close
    pp_entryBar := bar_index
    pp_flips    := pp_flips + 1
    // Structural stop = the source's opposing swing (fallback: 1.5 ATR); TP = R multiple
    pp_sl       := pp_srcDir == 1 ? (not na(pp_swL) and pp_swL < close ? pp_swL : close - atr * 1.5) : (not na(pp_swH) and pp_swH > close ? pp_swH : close + atr * 1.5)
    float _ppRisk = math.max(math.abs(close - pp_sl), atr * 0.1)
    pp_tp       := pp_srcDir == 1 ? close + _ppRisk * ppTpR : close - _ppRisk * ppTpR
    if ppShowSignals
        label.new(bar_index, pp_srcDir == 1 ? low - atr * 0.5 : high + atr * 0.5,
             pp_srcDir == 1 ? "PP BUY" : "PP SELL",
             style     = pp_srcDir == 1 ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
             color     = pp_srcDir == 1 ? color.new(color.teal, 0) : color.new(color.red, 0),
             textcolor = color.white, size = size.small)

bool pp_buySignal  = pp_flip and pp_srcDir == 1
bool pp_sellSignal = pp_flip and pp_srcDir == -1
alertcondition(pp_buySignal,  title="Ping Pong BUY",  message="Ping Pong: flip LONG")
alertcondition(pp_sellSignal, title="Ping Pong SELL", message="Ping Pong: flip SHORT")

// Floating points on the open leg
float pp_openPts = (ppMode and pp_pos != 0 and not na(pp_entry)) ? (close - pp_entry) * pp_pos : 0.0
int   pp_decided = pp_wins + pp_losses
float pp_winRate = pp_decided > 0 ? pp_wins / pp_decided * 100.0 : 0.0

// Current-trade Stop / Target lines (refreshed on last bar)
var line pp_slLine = na
var line pp_tpLine = na
if ppMode and barstate.islast
    if not na(pp_slLine)
        line.delete(pp_slLine)
    if not na(pp_tpLine)
        line.delete(pp_tpLine)
    if pp_pos != 0 and not na(pp_sl) and not na(pp_tp)
        pp_slLine := line.new(bar_index, pp_sl, bar_index + 30, pp_sl, color=color.red, width=1, style=line.style_dashed)
        pp_tpLine := line.new(bar_index, pp_tp, bar_index + 30, pp_tp, color=#00ff88, width=1, style=line.style_dashed)

// ── Ping Pong panel ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
if ppMode and ppShowPanel and barstate.islast and showTable
    var table ppT = table.new(position.top_center, 2, 10, bgcolor=color.new(#05050f, 0), border_color=color.new(color.fuchsia, 40), border_width=1, frame_color=color.new(color.fuchsia, 20), frame_width=1)
    string _ppsz = dashTextSize == "tiny" ? size.tiny : dashTextSize == "normal" ? size.normal : size.small
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 0, "PING PONG", text_color=color.black, bgcolor=color.new(color.fuchsia, 0), text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 0, pp_pos == 1 ? "LONG" : pp_pos == -1 ? "SHORT" : "FLAT", text_color=color.black, bgcolor=(pp_pos == 1 ? #00ff88 : pp_pos == -1 ? color.red : color.gray), text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 1, "Source", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 1, ppSource + (ppFilter ? " (filtered)" : ""), text_color=color.white, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 2, "Entry", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 2, na(pp_entry) ? "–" : str.tostring(math.round_to_mintick(pp_entry)), text_color=color.white, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 3, "Stop", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 3, na(pp_sl) ? "–" : str.tostring(math.round_to_mintick(pp_sl)), text_color=color.red, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 4, "Target", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 4, na(pp_tp) ? "–" : str.tostring(math.round_to_mintick(pp_tp)), text_color=#00ff88, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 5, "Open Leg", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 5, str.tostring(pp_openPts, "#.##") + " pts", text_color=(pp_openPts >= 0 ? #00ff88 : color.red), text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 6, "R:R", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 6, str.tostring(ppTpR, "#.#") + " : 1", text_color=color.white, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 7, "Flips", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 7, str.tostring(pp_flips), text_color=color.white, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 8, "Win %", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 8, pp_decided == 0 ? "–" : str.tostring(math.round(pp_winRate)) + "%  (" + str.tostring(pp_wins) + "/" + str.tostring(pp_decided) + ")", text_color=(pp_winRate >= 50 ? #00ff88 : color.red), text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 0, 9, "Cum Pts", text_color=color.fuchsia, text_size=_ppsz)
    table.cell(ppT, 1, 9, str.tostring(pp_cumPts, "#.##"), text_color=(pp_cumPts >= 0 ? #00ff88 : color.red), text_size=_ppsz)



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FRACTAL STACK ALERTS (v90) — fire when the fixed-layer stack flips or fully aligns.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
bool stackFlippedBull  = fractalStackDir == 1  and fractalStackDir[1] != 1
bool stackFlippedBear  = fractalStackDir == -1 and fractalStackDir[1] != -1
bool stackFullyAligned = fractalStackScore >= 100.0 and fractalStackScore[1] < 100.0
alertcondition(stackFlippedBull,  title="Fractal Stack flip BULL", message="Fractal stack flipped bullish")
alertcondition(stackFlippedBear,  title="Fractal Stack flip BEAR", message="Fractal stack flipped bearish")
alertcondition(stackFullyAligned, title="All Layers Aligned",      message="All fixed layers aligned (100% stack)")
